[SYSTEM]
You are an RTL (Register Transfer Level) design agent. Your task is to create a Verilog/SystemVerilog design that satisfies the given specification, is functionally correct, and has minimal cost (area_delay_product).

**Cost metric `area_delay_product`:** area × achieved critical-path delay — the classic area-delay product. Does NOT include simulation cycle count (contrast with the 'area_runtime_product' metric). Cost is measured on your design alone. The testbench and everything it owns (checking logic, the external memories, etc.) is not synthesized and contributes nothing to area, delay, power, or energy.

## Specification
Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (a Google-TPU-style MXU),
module name `tpu`, implementing the EXACT per-cycle behavior of the logikbench
`tpu` benchmark (zeroasiccorp/logikbench, MIT).

FUNCTION: computes C = A * B tile products on an 8x8 grid of processing
elements. B (the "weight" matrix, 8-bit signed) is loaded into the array and
held; A (the "activation" matrix, 8-bit signed) streams through; result rows
C (32-bit signed) drain out the bottom.

PROTOCOL:
  1. Weight load: assert w_valid for 8 cycles; w_data carries one weight row
     per cycle, 8 signed bytes packed { col 7 ... col 0 }, BOTTOM matrix row
     first (cycle k carries B[7-k][*]; the weights shift south). After 8
     cycles PE(i,j) holds B[i][j].
  2. Compute: assert a_valid while streaming A one row per cycle (packed the
     same way). Row m of the result, C[m][*] packed { col 7 ... col 0 } as 8
     signed 32-bit words, appears on c_data with c_valid high exactly 15
     (= 2*8-1) compute cycles after its A row was accepted.
  - The array advances only while w_valid is low: asserting w_valid stalls
    any in-flight computation (and reloads weights). Zeros are injected while
    a_valid is low, so idle/drain cycles add nothing to the accumulators.
  - Multiple A tiles may stream back-to-back against the same loaded weights.

CYCLE ACCURACY: c_valid is compared against the original design EVERY cycle;
c_data is compared on every cycle where c_valid is high. The 15-cycle
compute-latency and the stall semantics are part of the contract.

Ports:
  clk, rst (async active-high reset, asserted only before the stream begins)
  w_valid, w_data[63:0]
  a_valid, a_data[63:0]
  c_valid, c_data[255:0]

A working starting point is provided: `starting_point.v` is the
original logikbench RTL (tpu_pe / tpu_array / tpu: PE mesh, per-row input
skew, per-column output de-skew, valid pipeline). It passes every cycle of the golden
trace in `vectors.dat` (directed zero/identity/extreme tiles, random tiles,
back-to-back streams, a_valid gaps, mid-drain weight reloads, random soak).
Study it, then optimize while preserving the cycle-accurate behavior. The
sibling benchmark `tpu_spire` shares the identical testbench and vectors
with a SpireHDL starting point.

## Tools
- create_file: Create a new file in your working directory
- replace_file: Replace the entire contents of an existing file
- apply_diff: Apply a unified diff to modify a file
- edit_file: Edit a file by replacing an exact string match with new content. Provide old_string (must match exactly once) and new_string.
- ls: List files in your working directory
- read_file: Read the contents of a file
- run_evaluation(filename[, target_delay]): Run evaluation on the given design file. Returns correctness (Verilator simulation) + cost (Yosys area_delay_product). The filename must be your main design file (e.g. 'design.sv'). Optionally pass target_delay (ps) to override the synthesis timing constraint.

## Strategy
1. Lay out an action plan. Try to cover a diverse set of approaches in your plan to increase the chances of finding a good solution within the step limit. Also once you find a new best solution, explore close solutions. Trade off exploration with exploitation.
2. First, create a simple, straightforward design that is functionally correct. Focus on getting 100% correctness first.
3. Run evaluation to confirm correctness and get the initial cost (area_delay_product).
4. ONLY after achieving 100% correctness, try to optimize the design to reduce the area_delay_product.
5. After each optimization, run evaluation to verify correctness is maintained and check the new cost.
6. If an optimization breaks correctness, revert and try a different approach.
7. Keep iterating until you run out of steps — use every step to try improvements. The run ends automatically at the step limit; your best passing design is kept.

In general, be creative and take risk. At least after you have a correct design, try bold optimizations to reduce area_delay_product, even if they might break correctness. You can always revert if it doesn't work.
The best results you obtain which is 100% correctness counts.
Try to beat at least the straightforward implementation.

Running evaluation is crucial after every change to ensure correctness and track cost. Do not skip evaluations.

You may do some 'thinking' alongside with your tool calling responses to explain your reasoning, but always remember to call a tool in every response.

## Important
- Always call a tool in every response.
- You have a maximum of **60 steps** (LLM turns). Budget them wisely: get a correct design first, then optimise. You may batch tool calls in one response --- at most 8, and only one `run_evaluation`.
- The best result is the one with lowest cost among designs that pass 100% of testbench checks.
- Use different filenames for different design variants so you can retrieve them later.
- If context files (e.g. component source files) are in your workspace, you can modify them directly using edit_file or replace_file to optimize the design. Make targeted, incremental changes rather than rewriting files from scratch — small edits are less likely to introduce bugs.
- Write clean, synthesizable RTL code.
- You can pass `target_delay` (ps) to `run_evaluation` to try different synthesis timing constraints; experimenting with this may improve PPA results (lower target delay may reduce delay, higher target delay may reduce area/transistor count). target_delay must be > 0.
- Do not spend too many steps sweeping `target_delay` — basically if you want to minimize delay choose a low value,if you want to minimize area/transistor count choose a high value. That's it.A few targeted experiments are more productive than an exhaustive sweep.

## Delay Optimization Tips
- `target_delay` trades area against delay (a lower value pressures toward faster logic, a higher value lets Yosys re-map to smaller cells). Do not overly iterate on different target delays; often the RTL structure is the real lever, target_delay is only a minor secondary knob.
- After getting a correct design, try different accumulation orderings (sequential vs. tree) and compare results — Yosys synthesis can produce noticeably different delays (~10 ps) from structurally similar descriptions.
- For designs dominated by a multiply-accumulate chain, the critical path is the multiplier followed by the adder tree. Reducing the adder depth or splitting into parallel partial sums can help.

## Reference Implementations
The following are reference implementations for inspiration. Study them for patterns and techniques, but do not copy them directly — adapt ideas to your specific design.

### Parallel-prefix adder (ripple-carry / carry-lookahead) (Add.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Binary adder using ripple-carry or parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.
// S = A+B

module Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);

	// Function: Binary adder using parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.

	logic [width-1:0] GI, PI;  // prefix gen./prop. in
	logic [width-1:0] GO, PO;  // prefix gen./prop. out
	logic [width-1:0] PT;  // adder propagate temp

	// Internal signals for unsigned operands
	logic [width-1:0] Auns, Buns, Suns;

	// default ripple-carry adder as slow implementation
	if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin
		// type conversion: std_logic_vector -> unsigned
		assign Auns = A;
		assign Buns = B;

		// addition
		assign Suns = Auns + Buns;

		// type conversion: unsigned -> std_logic_vector
		assign S = Suns;
	end else begin
		// parallel-prefix adders as medium and fast implementations

		// calculate prefix input generate/propagate signals
		assign GI = A & B;
		assign PI = A | B;
		// calculate adder propagate signals (PT = A xor B)
		assign PT = ~GI & PI;

		// calculate prefix output generate/propagate signals
		PrefixAndOr #(
			.width(width),
			.speed(speed)
		) prefix (
			.GI(GI),
			.PI(PI),
			.GO(GO),
			.PO(PO)
		);

		// calculate sum bits
		assign S = PT ^ {GO[width-2:0], 1'b0};
	end
endmodule



module behavioural_Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);
	assign S = A + B;
endmodule
```

### Prefix AND-OR structures (Sklansky, Brent-Kung, serial) (PrefixAndOr.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Prefix structures of different depth (i.e. speed) for carry calculation
// in binary adders. Compute in m levels new generate/propagate signal pairs
// for always larger groups of bits. Generate signals of the last level correspond
// to carries in binary addition. Basic logic operations: AND-OR for generate
// signals, AND for propagate signals.

module PrefixAndOr #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] GI,  // gen./prop. in
	input  logic [width-1:0] PI,  // gen./prop. in
	output logic [width-1:0] GO,  // gen./prop. out
	output logic [width-1:0] PO   // gen./prop. out
);

	// Constants
	localparam int n = width;  // prefix structure width
	localparam int m = $clog2(width);  // prefix structure depth

	// Sklansky parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::FAST) begin : fastPrefix
		logic [(m+1)*n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
			assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
			assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;
			for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels
				for (genvar k = 0; k < 2 ** (m - l); k++) begin : groups
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2 ** (l - 1); i++) begin : bits
						// pass prop and gen to following nodes
						if ((k * 2 ** l + i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = GT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
							assign PT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = PT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
						end
						// calculate new propagate and generate
						if ((k * 2 ** l + 2 ** (l - 1) + i) < n) begin : black
							assign GT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
												GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
											  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												 & GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1] );
							assign PT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
													PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1];
						end
					end
				end
			end
			assign GO = GT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
			assign PO = PT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
	end

	// Brent-Kung parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::MEDIUM) begin : mediumPrefix
		logic [(2*m)*n -1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
		assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;

		for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels1
			for (genvar k = 0; k < 2**(m-l); k++) begin : groups
				for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**l -1; i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**l +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
					end // white
				end // bits
				if ((k* 2**l + 2**l -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l - 1] |
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							     & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**(l-1) -1]);
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) -1];
				end // black
			end
		end // level1
		for (genvar l = m +1; l < 2*m; l++) begin : levels2
			for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
				if (i < n) begin : white
					assign GT[l*n +i] = GT[(l-1)*n +i];
					assign PT[l*n +i] = PT[(l-1)*n +i];
				end // white
			end // bits
			for (genvar k = 1; k < 2**(l-m); k++) begin : groups
				if (l < 2*m -1) begin : empty
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l -1) -1; i++) begin : bits
						if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
							assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						end // white
					end
				end // empty
				if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l -1) -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m-l-1) -1] 
								 & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) -1] );
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) -1];
				end // black
				for (genvar i = 2**(2*m -l-1); i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
					end // white
				end
			end
		end // level2
		assign GO = GT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
		assign PO = PT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
	end  // Serial-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	else if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin : slowPrefix
		logic [n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[0] = GI[0];
		assign PT[0] = PI[0];
		
		for (genvar i = 1; i < n; i++) begin : bits
			assign GT[i] = GI[i] | (PI[i] & GT[i-1]);
			assign PT[i] = PI[i] & PT[i-1];
		end
		assign GO = GT;
		assign PO = PT;
	end

endmodule

```

[Step 2/60]
//#############################################################################
// Copyright: Zero ASIC. All rights Reserved.
// Author: Andreas Olofsson
// License:  MIT (see LICENSE file in LogikBench repository)
//#############################################################################
//
// Processing element (PE) for the weight-stationary systolic array (TPU MXU).
//
// Each PE holds one stationary signed weight 'w'. Two independent datapaths
// share the cell:
//
//   Weight load (ld): the weights form a per-column shift register that loads
//   from the top. On each ld cycle the PE captures the weight from the PE above
//   (w_north) and presents its stored weight downward (w_south). After N load
//   cycles every PE in a column holds its final stationary weight.
//
//   Compute (en): the activation flows west to east (registered, one hop per
//   cycle) and the partial sum flows north to south, accumulating the local
//   product a_west * w. This is the only arithmetic cell in the array: one
//   signed DW x DW multiply plus an ACCW-bit add.
//
//#############################################################################

module tpu_pe
  #(parameter DW = 8,	 // operand width (signed)
    parameter ACCW = 32) // accumulator / partial-sum width (signed)
   (
    input		  clk,
    input		  rst,	      // synchronous, active high
    input		  ld,	      // weight-load shift enable
    input		  en,	      // compute enable
    input [DW-1:0]	  w_north,    // weight in from PE above
    input [DW-1:0]	  a_west,     // activation in from west
    input [ACCW-1:0]	  psum_north, // partial sum in from above
    output [DW-1:0]	  w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below
    output reg [DW-1:0]	  a_east,     // activation out to east
    output reg [ACCW-1:0] psum_south  // partial sum out to below
    );

   // stationary weight register; presented downward for the load shift chain
   reg [DW-1:0] w;
   assign w_south = w;

   // local product: the ONLY signed operation. Operands are interpreted as
   // signed here via $signed; the 2*DW-bit result holds the two's-complement
   // product, which is then sign-extended to the accumulator width below.
   wire [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);

   always @(posedge clk) begin
      if (rst) begin
         w          <= {DW{1'b0}};
         a_east     <= {DW{1'b0}};
         psum_south <= {ACCW{1'b0}};
      end
      else begin
         if (ld)
           w <= w_north;
         if (en) begin
            a_east     <= a_west;
            psum_south <= psum_north +
                          {{(ACCW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};
         end
      end
   end

endmodule
//#############################################################################
// Copyright: Zero ASIC. All rights Reserved.
// Author: Andreas Olofsson
// License:  MIT (see LICENSE file in LogikBench repository)
//#############################################################################
//
// N x N weight-stationary systolic array (the TPU MXU core).
//
// One tpu_pe per grid element, wired as a 2D mesh (per CLAUDE.md: replicated
// hardware via generate, never a procedural unroll):
//
//   - weights load from the top: w_top[j] feeds column j; each column is a
//     vertical shift register (w_south of PE(i,j) -> w_north of PE(i+1,j)).
//   - activations flow west to east: a_left[i] enters row i; a_east of PE(i,j)
//     feeds a_west of PE(i,j+1).
//   - partial sums flow north to south and accumulate; the top row's psum_north
//     is tied to zero (fresh accumulation), and c_bot[j] is the column-j result
//     leaving the bottom row.
//
// With weight B[i][j] held at PE(i,j) and activation A[m][i] entering row i,
// column j computes C[m][j] = sum_i A[m][i] * B[i][j]. Input/output skew (which
// time-aligns rows and columns) lives in the tpu top, not here.
//
//#############################################################################

module tpu_array
  #(parameter N = 8,	 // array dimension (N x N PEs)
    parameter DW = 8,	 // operand width (signed)
    parameter ACCW = 32) // accumulator width (signed)
   (
    input		clk,
    input		rst,	// synchronous, active high
    input		ld,	// weight-load shift enable
    input		en,	// compute enable
    input [N*DW-1:0]	w_top,	// weight inputs, one per column (load)
    input [N*DW-1:0]	a_left,	// activation inputs, one per row
    output [N*ACCW-1:0]	c_bot	// column results, one per column
    );

   // mesh nets: a_h carries activations east, psum_v carries sums south,
   // w_v carries weights south during load.
   wire [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];
   wire [ACCW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];
   wire [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];

   genvar	   i, j;
   generate
      // west edge: activation inputs into column 0 of each row
      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west
         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];
      end
      // top edge: zero partial sums, weight inputs; bottom edge: results out

... (showing lines 1-118 of 282; use offset/limit for more)

[Step 2/60]
// Self-checking testbench for the tpu benchmark (8x8 weight-stationary
// systolic matmul tile). Reads vectors.dat, one line per clock cycle:
//   w_valid w_data(64b) a_valid a_data(64b)   c_valid c_data(256b)   (hex)
// The expected outputs are the ORIGINAL logikbench tpu's per-cycle behavior;
// c_valid and c_data are compared CYCLE-ACCURATELY every cycle (c_data only
// while c_valid is high — the original holds stale data between results).
module tb;
  localparam N = 8, DW = 8, ACCW = 32;
  int total_checks;
  int total_errors;

  logic clk, rst;
  logic w_valid, a_valid, c_valid;
  logic [N*DW-1:0] w_data, a_data;
  logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_data;

  tpu dut
    (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .w_valid(w_valid), .w_data(w_data),
     .a_valid(a_valid), .a_data(a_data), .c_valid(c_valid), .c_data(c_data));

  initial clk = 0;
  always #5 clk = ~clk;

  integer fd, rc, line_num;
  string line_buf;
  logic s_wv, s_av, e_cv;
  logic [63:0] s_wd, s_ad;
  logic [255:0] e_cd;

  initial begin
    total_checks = 0;
    total_errors = 0;
    w_valid = 0; a_valid = 0; w_data = 0; a_data = 0;
    rst = 1;
    repeat (4) @(negedge clk);
    rst = 0;

    fd = $fopen("vectors.dat", "r");
    if (fd == 0) begin
      $display("ERROR: cannot open vectors.dat");
      $fatal(1);
    end
    line_num = 0;
    while (!$feof(fd)) begin
      line_num = line_num + 1;
      void'($fgets(line_buf, fd));
      if (line_buf.len() == 0) continue;
      if (line_buf.substr(0, 0) == "#") continue;
      rc = $sscanf(line_buf, "%h %h %h %h %h %h", s_wv, s_wd, s_av, s_ad, e_cv, e_cd);
      if (rc != 6) continue;
      w_valid = s_wv; w_data = s_wd; a_valid = s_av; a_data = s_ad;
      @(negedge clk);
      total_checks = total_checks + 1;
      if (c_valid !== e_cv || (e_cv && c_data !== e_cd)) begin
        $display("TB_ERROR line=%0d exp(cv=%b cd=%h) got(cv=%b cd=%h)",
                 line_num, e_cv, e_cd, c_valid, c_data);
        total_errors = total_errors + 1;
        if (total_errors > 20) begin
          $display("TB_SUMMARY total=%0d errors=%0d", total_checks, total_errors);
          $fatal(1, "FAIL (too many errors, aborting)");
        end
      end
    end
    $fclose(fd);

    $display("TB_SUMMARY total=%0d errors=%0d", total_checks, total_errors);
    if (total_errors != 0) $fatal(1, "FAIL");
    $display("PASS");
    $finish;
  end
endmodule

//#############################################################################
// Copyright: Zero ASIC. All rights Reserved.
// Author: Andreas Olofsson
// License:  MIT (see LICENSE file in LogikBench repository)
//#############################################################################
//
// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (a Google-TPU-style MXU).
//
// Computes one tile product C = A * B, where A and B are N x N signed operands
// (DW bits) and C is N x N signed (ACCW bits). B is the "weight" matrix, loaded
// into and held by the array; A is the "activation" matrix, streamed through; C
// drains out the bottom. Built from a tpu_array of N x N tpu_pe cells.
//
// Operation has two phases the host drives via valids:
//
//   1. Weight load: assert w_valid for N cycles. w_data carries one weight row
//      per cycle, N signed weights packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }. Because the
//      array loads top-down (shift south), push the BOTTOM matrix row first:
//      cycle k carries B[N-1-k][*]. After N cycles PE(i,j) holds B[i][j].
//
//   2. Compute: assert a_valid while streaming A one row per cycle, a_data
//      packed { col N-1 ... col 0 } = A[m][N-1..0]. Row m of the result, C[m][*]
//      (packed the same way), appears on c_data with c_valid high, 2*N-1 cycles
//      after its A row was accepted.
//
// Time alignment: row i of A is delayed i cycles entering the array (input
// skew) so partial sums chain correctly down each column; column j of the
// result is delayed N-1-j cycles (output de-skew) so a full result row leaves
// together. Both skew networks and the valid pipeline advance only with the
// array (compute enable), so the 2*N-1 latency is exact. Per CLAUDE.md the skew
// chains are generated per-row/column shift registers, not a procedural unroll.
//
//#############################################################################

module tpu
  #(parameter N = 8,	 // array dimension (N x N)
    parameter DW = 8,	 // operand width (signed)
    parameter ACCW = 32) // result / accumulator width (signed)
   (
    input		clk,
    input		rst,	 // synchronous, active high
    input		w_valid, // weight-row valid (load phase)
    input [N*DW-1:0]	w_data,	 // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }
    input		a_valid, // activation-row valid (compute phase)
    input [N*DW-1:0]	a_data,	 // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }
    output		c_valid, // result-row valid
    output [N*ACCW-1:0]	c_data	 // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }
    );

   // total injection-to-output latency, in compute cycles
   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;

   // load weights while w_valid; otherwise the array computes (and drains)
   wire	      ld = w_valid;
   wire	      en = ~w_valid;

   //##########################################################################
   // Input skew: row i of the activation is delayed i cycles. Zero is injected
   // when a_valid is low so idle/drain cycles add nothing to the accumulators.
   //##########################################################################
   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;
   genvar	   i;
   generate
      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew
         wire [DW-1:0] din =
		       a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};
         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0
            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;
         end
         else begin : g_skewn
            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];
            integer k;
            always @(posedge clk) begin
               if (rst) begin
                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)
                    sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};
               end
               else if (en) begin
                  sr[0] <= din;
                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)
                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];
               end
            end
            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];
         end
      end
   endgenerate

   //##########################################################################
   // The systolic array.
   //##########################################################################
   wire [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot;
   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_array
     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),
      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));

   //##########################################################################
   // Output de-skew: column j is delayed N-1-j cycles so all columns of a
   // result row line up. Column N-1 (longest path) needs no delay.
   //##########################################################################
   genvar j;
   generate
      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew
         localparam D = N - 1 - j;
         wire [ACCW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW];
         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0
            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = cin;
         end
         else begin : g_deskewn
            reg [ACCW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];
            integer k;
            always @(posedge clk) begin
               if (rst) begin
                  for (k = 0; k < D; k = k + 1)
                    dr[k] <= {ACCW{1'b0}};
               end
               else if (en) begin
                  dr[0] <= cin;
                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)
                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];
               end
            end
            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = dr[D-1];
         end
      end
   endgenerate

   //##########################################################################
   // Valid pipeline: c_valid is a_valid delayed LAT compute cycles.
   //##########################################################################
   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;
   always @(posedge clk) begin
      if (rst)
        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};
      else if (en)
        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};
   end
   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];

endmodule

[ASSISTANT]
Baseline: **ADP 4,624,409** (area 4604, delay 1004ps). Critical path is mul → 32-bit add → psum register. Key observation: psums never hold more than 8 products (each PE adds one product, chain of 8), so 19 signed bits suffice — the upper 13 bits of every 32-bit accumulator are pure sign bits. Let me build a narrowed variant.
  -> create_file(filename='design.sv', content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU) - optimized.\n// Cycle-accurate match to logikbench tpu.\n// Optimization: internal partial-sum datapath narrowed from 32 to PW bits.\n// Every psum in the array is a sum of at most N=8 products of 8x8 signed\n// operands (range [-130048, +131072]) which fits in 19 signed bits, so the\n// 32-bit output is recovered exactly by sign extension.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\t  // operand width (signed)\n    parameter PW = 19)\t  // internal partial-sum width (signed)\n   (\n    input\t\t  clk,\n    input\t\t  rst,\t      // synchronous, active high\n    input\t\t  ld,\t      // weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\t  en,\t      // compute enable\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  w_north,    // weight in from PE above\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  a_west,     // activation in from west\n    input [PW-1:0]\t  psum_north, // partial sum in from above\n    output [DW-1:0]\t  w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below\n    output reg [DW-1:0]\t  a_east,     // activation out to east\n    output reg [PW-1:0]\t  psum_south  // partial sum out to below\n    );\n\n   // stationary weight register; presented downward for the load shift chain\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // local product: signed DW x DW -> 2*DW bits, sign-extended to PW\n   wire [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         w          <= {DW{1'b0}};\n         a_east     <= {DW{1'b0}};\n         psum_south <= {PW{1'b0}};\n      end\n      else begin\n         if (ld)\n           w <= w_north;\n         if (en) begin\n            a_east     <= a_west;\n            psum_south <= psum_north +\n                          {{(PW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n         end\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter PW = 19)\n   (\n    input\t\t clk,\n    input\t\t rst,\n    input\t\t ld,\n    input\t\t en,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t w_top,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t a_left,\n    output [N*PW-1:0]\t c_bot\n    );\n\n   wire [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [PW-1:0]   psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar\t\t   i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign psum_v[0][j] = {PW{1'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot[j*PW +: PW] = psum_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .PW(PW)) u_pe\n              (.clk        (clk),\n               .rst        (rst),\n               .ld         (ld),\n               .en         (en),\n               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n               .psum_north (psum_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32,\t // result width (signed)\n    parameter PW = 19)\t\t // internal partial-sum width (signed)\n   (\n    input\t\t clk,\n    input\t\t rst,\n    input\t\t w_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t w_data,\n    input\t\t a_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t a_data,\n    output\t\t c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\t c_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire\t      ld = w_valid;\n   wire\t      en = ~w_valid;\n\n   // input skew: row i delayed i cycles, zeros injected when idle\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar\t   i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din =\n\t\t       a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   wire [N*PW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .PW(PW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   // output de-skew: column j delayed N-1-j compute cycles\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [PW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*PW +: PW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n                    {{(ACCW-PW){cin[PW-1]}}, cin};\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [PW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; [ASSISTANT]
The target_delay knob barely moves area (FF-dominated). Structural change needed: let me try a **carry-save (redundant) psum representation** — each PE stage becomes a single MAJ/XOR level instead of a 19-bit ripple adder, moving the carry-propagate addition out of the clock path to the column outputs.
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU) - carry-save variant.\n// Cycle-accurate match to logikbench tpu.\n//\n// Optimization vs baseline:\n//  - internal psum narrowed from 32 to PW=19 bits (<= 8 products of 8x8 signed)\n//  - psums kept in REDUNDANT (carry-save) form {carry, sum}: each PE stage is\n//    a single XOR3/MAJ level instead of a ripple adder, so the carry-propagate\n//    add leaves the clock-critical path. The CPA happens once per column at\n//    the array output (before the de-skew registers).\n//  All arithmetic is mod 2^PW, and the true accumulated value always fits in\n//  PW signed bits, so the final 32-bit result is recovered exactly.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter PW = 19)\n   (\n    input\t\t clk,\n    input\t\t rst,\t      // synchronous, active high\n    input\t\t ld,\t      // weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\t en,\t      // compute enable\n    input [DW-1:0]\t w_north,    // weight in from PE above\n    input [DW-1:0]\t a_west,     // activation in from west\n    input [2*PW-1:0]\t psum_north, // {carry,sum} partial sum in from above\n    output [DW-1:0]\t w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below\n    output reg [DW-1:0]\t a_east,     // activation out to east\n    output reg [2*PW-1:0] psum_south  // {carry,sum} partial sum out below\n    );\n\n   // stationary weight register; presented downward for the load shift chain\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // local product, sign-extended to PW bits\n   wire [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n   wire [PW-1:0]   p    = {{(PW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n\n   // carry-save accumulate: {ps,pc} = p + ns + nc  (mod 2^PW)\n   wire [PW-1:0] ns = psum_north[PW-1:0];\n   wire [PW-1:0] nc = psum_north[2*PW-1:PW];\n   wire [PW-1:0] ps = p ^ ns ^ nc;\n   wire [PW-1:0] pc = ((p & ns) | (p & nc) | (ns & nc)) << 1;\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         w          <= {DW{1'b0}};\n         a_east     <= {DW{1'b0}};\n         psum_south <= {(2*PW){1'b0}};\n      end\n      else begin\n         if (ld)\n           w <= w_north;\n         if (en) begin\n            a_east     <= a_west;\n            psum_south <= {pc, ps};\n         end\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter PW = 19)\n   (\n    input\t\t clk,\n    input\t\t rst,\n    input\t\t ld,\n    input\t\t en,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t w_top,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t a_left,\n    output [N*2*PW-1:0]\t c_bot\n    );\n\n   wire [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [2*PW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar\t   i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign psum_v[0][j] = {(2*PW){1'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot[j*2*PW +: 2*PW] = psum_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .PW(PW)) u_pe\n              (.clk        (clk),\n               .rst        (rst),\n               .ld         (ld),\n               .en         (en),\n               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n               .psum_north (psum_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32,\n    parameter PW = 19)\n   (\n    input\t\t clk,\n    input\t\t rst,\n    input\t\t w_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t w_data,\n    input\t\t a_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t a_data,\n    output\t\t c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\t c_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire\t      ld = w_valid;\n   wire\t      en = ~w_valid;\n\n   // input skew: row i delayed i cycles, zeros injected when idle\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar\t   i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din =\n\t\t       a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   wire [N*2*PW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .PW(PW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   // output de-skew: column j delayed N-1-j compute cycles.\n   // The carry-save -> binary CPA happens here, once per column, before the\n   // de-skew pipeline registers.\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [2*PW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*2*PW +: 2*PW];\n         wire [PW-1:0]   bin = cin[PW-1:0] + cin[2*PW-1:PW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n                    {{(ACCW-PW){bin[PW-1]}}, bin};\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [PW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= {PW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= bin;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n                    {{(ACCW-PW){dr[D-1][PW-1]}}, dr[D-1]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // valid pipeline: c_valid = a_valid delayed LAT compute cycles\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design2.sv')

[ASSISTANT]
target_delay has no effect (identical netlist). The 8-deep XOR chain hints Yosys may be widening the signed multiply with sign-correction logic. Let me force a compact explicit unsigned 8×8 multiply with modular sign correction.
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU) - variant 3.\n// Same cycle-accurate behavior as design2 (carry-save psums), but the local\n// product is formed as an UNSIGNED 8x8 multiply plus modular sign correction:\n//    a_s*w_s = (a_u - 256*a7)*(w_u - 256*w7)\n//            = a_u*w_u - (a7 ? w_u<<8 : 0) - (w7 ? a_u<<8 : 0)  (mod 2^16)\n// which keeps the multiplier array at 8x8 unsigned (no widened signed mul).\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter PW = 19)\n   (\n    input\t\t clk,\n    input\t\t rst,\n    input\t\t ld,\n    input\t\t en,\n    input [DW-1:0]\t w_north,\n    input [DW-1:0]\t a_west,\n    input [2*PW-1:0]\t psum_north,\n    output [DW-1:0]\t w_south,\n    output reg [DW-1:0]\t a_east,\n    output reg [2*PW-1:0] psum_south\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // signed product via unsigned 8x8 multiply + modular sign correction\n   wire [2*DW-1:0] u     = {8'h0, a_west} * {8'h0, w};\n   wire [2*DW-1:0] corr  = (a_west[DW-1] ? {w, 8'h0}     : {16{1'b0}})\n                         + (w[DW-1]      ? {a_west, 8'h0} : {16{1'b0}});\n   wire [2*DW-1:0] prod  = u - corr;\n\n   wire [PW-1:0] p = {{(PW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n\n   // carry-save accumulate: {ps,pc} = p + ns + nc  (mod 2^PW)\n   wire [PW-1:0] ns = psum_north[PW-1:0];\n   wire [PW-1:0] nc = psum_north[2*PW-1:PW];\n   wire [PW-1:0] ps = p ^ ns ^ nc;\n   wire [PW-1:0] pc = ((p & ns) | (p & nc) | (ns & nc)) << 1;\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         w [ASSISTANT]
design3 is worse (3.43M) — revert to design2 as best. Now a bolder attempt: replace `$signed*a` with a **manual partial-product compressor tree** that folds the psum operands (ns, nc) directly into the tree — eliminating both the multiplier's internal carry-propagate adder and the post-mul XOR/MAJ from the critical path.
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU) - variant 4.\n// Cycle-accurate behavior identical to design2.\n//\n// Key idea: no `*` operator. The product a*w is built from shifted rows\n//    a*w = sum_{k=0..6} w[k]*(a<<k) - w[7]*(a<<7)        (exact integer id)\n//    -ex = ~ex + 1  (mod 2^PW)\n// and the partial-sum operands {ns,nc} plus the constant row are folded into\n// ONE balanced 3:2 compressor tree. The PE stage therefore contains NO\n// carry-propagate adder at all: output is a redundant pair {pc,ps}.\n// All arithmetic mod 2^PW; true values fit PW=19 signed bits.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter PW = 19)\n   (\n    input\t\t clk,\n    input\t\t rst,\n    input\t\t ld,\n    input\t\t en,\n    input [DW-1:0]\t w_north,\n    input [DW-1:0]\t a_west,\n    input [2*PW-1:0]\t psum_north,\t// {carry,sum}\n    output [DW-1:0]\t w_south,\n    output reg [DW-1:0]\t a_east,\n    output reg [2*PW-1:0] psum_south\t// {carry,sum}\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // 3:2 compressor: {carry, sum} = x + y + z (mod 2^PW)\n   function [2*PW-1:0] csa3;\n      input [PW-1:0] x, y, z;\n      reg [PW-1:0]   sum, cry;\n      begin\n\t sum = x ^ y ^ z;\n\t cry = ((x & y) | (y & z) | (x & z)) << 1;\n\t csa3 = {cry, sum};\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   // shifted rows of the sign-extended activation\n   wire [PW-1:0] as = {{(PW-DW){a_west[DW-1]}}, a_west};\n   wire [PW-1:0] r0 = w[0] ? as              : {PW{1'b0}};\n   wire [PW-1:0] r1 = w[1] ? (as << 1)       : {PW{1'b0}};\n   wire [PW-1:0] r2 = w[2] ? (as << 2)       : {PW{1'b0}};\n   wire [PW-1:0] r3 = w[3] ? (as << 3)       : {PW{1'b0}};\n   wire [PW-1:0] r4 = w[4] ? (as << 4)       : {PW{1'b0}};\n   wire [PW-1:0] r5 = w[5] ? (as << 5)       : {PW{1'b0}};\n   wire [PW-1:0] r6 = w[6] ? (as << 6)       : {PW{1'b0}};\n   wire [PW-1:0] ex = w[7] ? (as << 7)       : {PW{1'b0}};\n   wire [PW-1:0] nx = ~ex;\t\t\t// -ex = ~ex + 1\n   wire [PW-1:0] one = {{(PW-1){1'b0}}, 1'b1};\n\n   wire [PW-1:0] ns = psum_north[PW-1:0];\n   wire [PW-1:0] nc = psum_north[2*PW-1:PW];\n\n   // level 0: 9 rows -> 3 pairs\n   wire [2*PW-1:0] t0 = csa3(r0, r1, r2);\n   wire [2*PW-1:0] t1 = csa3(r3, r4, r5);\n   wire [2*PW-1:0] t2 = csa3(r6, nx, one);\n   // level 1: 6 + {ns,nc} -> 4\n   wire [2*PW-1:0] u0 = csa3(t0[PW-1:0], t0[2*PW-1:PW], t1[PW-1:0]);\n   wire [2*PW-1:0] u1 = csa3(t1[2*PW-1:PW], t2[PW-1:0], t2[2*PW-1:PW]);\n   // level 2\n   wire [2*PW-1:0] v0 = csa3(u0[PW-1:0], u0[2*PW-1:PW], u1[PW-1:0]);\n   wire [2*PW-1:0] v1 = csa3(u1[2*PW-1:PW], ns, nc);\n   // level 3\n   wire [2*PW-1:0] f0 = csa3(v0[PW-1:0], v0[2*PW-1:PW], v1[PW-1:0]);\n   // level 4: final redundant pair\n   wire [2*PW-1:0] f1 = csa3(f0[PW-1:0], f0[2*PW-1:PW], v1[2*PW-1:PW]);\n\n   wire [PW-1:0] ps = f1[PW-1:0];\n   wire [PW-1:0] pc = f1[2*PW-1:PW];\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n\t w          <= {DW{1'b0}};\n\t a_east     <= {DW{1'b0}};\n\t psum_south <= {(2*PW){1'b0}};\n      end\n      else begin\n\t if (ld)\n\t   w <= w_north;\n\t if (en) begin\n\t    a_east     <= a_west;\n\t    psum_south <= {pc, ps};\n\t end\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter PW = 19)\n   (\n    input\t\t clk,\n    input\t\t rst,\n    input\t\t ld,\n    input\t\t en,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t w_top,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t a_left,\n    output [N*2*PW-1:0]\t c_bot\n    );\n\n   wire [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [2*PW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar\t   i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; Using an asynchronous\n// reset removes the reset mux from the D-path of every flip-flop, which sits\n// at the end of the clock-critical path.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter PW = 19)\n   (\n    input\t\t clk,\n    input\t\t rst,\t\t// async, active high\n    input\t\t ld,\n    input\t\t en,\n    input [DW-1:0]\t w_north,\n    input [DW-1:0]\t a_west,\n    input [2*PW-1:0]\t psum_north,\n    output [DW-1:0]\t w_south,\n    output reg [DW-1:0]\t a_east,\n    output reg [2*PW-1:0] psum_south\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   function [2*PW-1:0] csa3;\n      input [PW-1:0] x, y, z;\n      reg [PW-1:0]   sum, cry;\n      begin\n\t sum = x ^ y ^ z;\n\t cry = ((x & y) | (y & z) | (x & z)) << 1;\n\t csa3 = {cry, sum};\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   wire [PW-1:0] as = {{(PW-DW){a_west[DW-1]}}, a_west};\n   wire [PW-1:0] r0 = w[0] ? as              : {PW{1'b0}};\n   wire [PW-1:0] r1 = w[1] ? (as << 4)       : {PW{1'b0}};\n   wire [PW-1:0] r5 = w[5] ? (as << 5)       : {PW{1'b0}};\n   wire [PW-1:0] r6 = w[6] ? (as << 6)       : {PW{1'b0}};\n   wire [PW-1:0] ex = w[7] ? [ASSISTANT]
**New best: design6 @300 = 2.495M** (delay 510, area 4887). Now a luck/structure probe: same math, different operand grouping in the compressor chain (puts ns/nc at level 0, nx at the final level):
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU) - variant 7.\n// Same behavior/datapath as design6 (no datapath reset, carry-save psums,\n// manual partial-product compressor). Only the GROUPING of operands in the\n// 3:2 compressor chain differs (ns/nc enter at level 0, the negated bit-7\n// row joins at the last level), which changes the logic structure handed to\n// the mapper.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter PW = 19)\n   (\n    input\t\t clk,\n    input\t\t rst,\n    input\t\t ld,\n    input\t\t en,\n    input [DW-1:0]\t w_north,\n    input [DW-1:0]\t a_west,\n    input [2*PW-1:0]\t psum_north,\n    output [DW-1:0]\t w_south,\n    output reg [DW-1:0]\t a_east,\n    output reg [2*PW-1:0] psum_south\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   function [2*PW-1:0] csa3;\n      input [PW-1:0] x, y, z;\n      reg [PW-1:0]   sum, cry;\n      begin\n\t sum = x ^ y ^ z;\n\t cry = ((x & y) | (y & z) | (x & z)) << 1;\n\t csa3 = {cry, sum};\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   wire [PW-1:0] as = {{(PW-DW){a_west[DW-1]}}, a_west};\n   wire [PW-1:0] r0 = w[0] ? as              : {PW{1'b0}};\n   wire [PW-1:0] r1 = w[1] ? (as << 1)       : {PW{1'b0}};\n   wire [PW-1:0] r2 = w[2] ? (as << 2)       : {PW{1'b0}};\n   wire [PW-1:0] r3 = w[3] ? (as << 3)       : {PW{1'b0}};\n   wire [PW-1:0] r4 = w[4] ? (as << 4)       : {PW{1'b0}};\n   wire [PW-1:0] r5 = w[5] ? (as << 5)       : {PW{1'b0}};\n   wire [PW-1:0] r6 = w[6] ? (as << 6)       : {PW{1'b0}};\n   wire [PW-1:0] ex = w[7] ? (as << 7)       : {PW{1'b0}};\n   wire [PW-1:0] nx = ~ex;\t\t\t// -ex = ~ex + 1 (const folds in)\n   wire [PW-1:0] one = {{(PW-1){1'b0}}, 1'b1};\n\n   wire [PW-1:0] ns = psum_north[PW-1:0];\n   wire [PW-1:0] nc = psum_north[2*PW-1:PW];\n\n   // level 0: psum pair + rows 0..6\n   wire [2*PW-1:0] t0 = csa3(ns, nc, r0);\n   wire [2*PW-1:0] t1 = csa3(r1, r2, r3);\n   wire [2*PW-1:0] t2 = csa3(r4, r5, r6);\n   // level 1\n   wire [2*PW-1:0] u0 = csa3(t0[PW-1:0], t0[2*PW-1:PW], t1[PW-1:0]);\n   wire [2*PW-1:0] u1 = csa3(t1[2*PW-1:PW], t2[PW-1:0], t2[2*PW-1:PW]);\n   // level 2 (nx still pending)\n   wire [2*PW-1:0] v0 = csa3(u0[PW-1:0], u0[2*PW-1:PW], u1[PW-1:0]);\n   // level 3\n   wire [2*PW-1:0] f0 = csa3(v0[PW-1:0], v0[2*PW-1:PW], u1[2*PW-1:PW]);\n   // level 4: fold nx and the constant +1\n   wire [2*PW-1:0] f1 = csa3(f0[PW-1:0], f0[2*PW-1:PW], nx ^ one);\n\n   wire [PW-1:0] ps = f1[PW-1:0];\n   wire [PW-1:0] pc = f1[2*PW-1:PW];\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (ld)\n\tw <= w_north;\n      if (en) begin\n\t a_east     <= a_west;\n\t psum_south <= {pc, ps};\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter PW = 19)\n   (\n    input\t\t clk,\n    input\t\t rst,\n    input\t\t ld,\n    input\t\t en,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t w_top,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t a_left,\n    output [N*2*PW-1:0]\t c_bot\n    );\n\n   wire [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [2*PW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar\t   i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n\t assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n\t assign psum_v[0][j] = {(2*PW){1'b0}};\n\t assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n\t assign c_bot[j*2*PW +: 2*PW] = psum_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n\t for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n\t    tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .PW(PW)) u_pe\n\t      (.clk        (clk),\n\t       .rst        (rst),\n\t       .ld         (ld),\n\t       .en         (en),\n\t       .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n\t       .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n\t       .psum_north (psum_v[i][j]),\n\t       .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n\t       .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n\t       .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j]));\n\t end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32,\n    parameter PW = 19)\n   (\n    input\t\t clk,\n    input\t\t rst,\n    input\t\t w_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t w_data,\n    input\t\t a_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t a_data,\n    output\t\t c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\t c_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire\t      ld = w_valid;\n   wire\t      en = ~w_valid;\n\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar\t   i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n\t wire [DW-1:0] din =\n\t\t\ta_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n\t if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n\t    assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n\t end\n\t else begin : g_skewn\n\t    reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n\t    integer k;\n\t    always @(posedge clk) begin\n\t       if (en) begin\n\t\t  sr[0] <= din;\n\t\t  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n\t\t    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n\t       end\n\t    end\n\t    assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n\t end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   wire [N*2*PW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .PW(PW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n\t localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n\t wire [2*PW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*2*PW +: 2*PW];\n\t wire [PW-1:0]   bin = cin[PW-1:0] + cin[2*PW-1:PW];\n\t if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n\t    assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t\t    {{(ACCW-PW){bin[PW-1]}}, bin};\n\t end\n\t else begin : g_deskewn\n\t    reg [PW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n\t    integer k;\n\t    always @(posedge clk) begin\n\t       if (en) begin\n\t\t  dr[0] <= bin;\n\t\t  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n\t\t    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n\t       end\n\t    end\n\t    assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t\t    {{(ACCW-PW){dr[D-1][PW-1]}}, dr[D-1]};\n\t end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n\tvpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n\tvpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design7.sv')

  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU) - variant 8.\n// Same behavior as design4: synchronous reset everywhere (like the original\n// logikbench RTL), carry-save psums, manual partial-product compressor.\n// Grouping variant B: psum pair + constant at level 0, rows 1..6 next,\n// rows 0 and the negated bit-7 row join at level 2.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter PW = 19)\n   (\n    input\t\t clk,\n    input\t\t rst,\n    input\t\t ld,\n    input\t\t en,\n    input [DW-1:0]\t w_north,\n    input [DW-1:0]\t a_west,\n    input [2*PW-1:0]\t psum_north,\n    output [DW-1:0]\t w_south,\n    output reg [DW-1:0]\t a_east,\n    output reg [2*PW-1:0] psum_south\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   function [2*PW-1:0] csa3;\n      input [PW-1:0] x, y, z;\n      reg [PW-1:0]   sum, cry;\n      begin\n\t sum = x ^ y ^ z;\n\t cry = ((x & y) | (y & z) | (x & z)) << 1;\n\t csa3 = {cry, sum};\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   wire [PW-1:0] as = {{(PW-DW){a_west[DW-1]}}, a_west};\n   wire [PW-1:0] r0 = w[0] ? as              : {PW{1'b0}};\n   wire [PW-1:0] r1 = w[1] ? (as << 1)       : {PW{1'b0}};\n   wire [PW-1:0] r2 = w[2] ? (as << 2)       : {PW{1'b0}};\n   wire [PW-1:0] r3 = w[3] ? (as << 3)       : {PW{1'b0}};\n   wire [PW-1:0] r4 = w[4] ? (as << 4)       : {PW{1'b0}};\n   wire [PW-1:0] r5 = w[5] ? (as << 5)       : {PW{1'b0}};\n   wire [PW-1:0] r6 = w[6] ? (as << 6)       : {PW{1'b0}};\n   wire [PW-1:0] ex = w[7] ? (as << 7)       : {PW{1'b0}};\n   wire [PW-1:0] nx = ~ex;\n   wire [PW-1:0] one = {{(PW-1){1'b0}}, 1'b1};\n\n   wire [PW-1:0] ns = psum_north[PW-1:0];\n   wire [PW-1:0] nc = psum_north[2*PW-1:PW];\n\n   // level 0\n   wire [2*PW-1:0] t0 = csa3(ns, nc, one);\n   wire [2*PW-1:0] t1 = csa3(r1, r2, r3);\n   wire [2*PW-1:0] t2 = csa3(r4, r5, r6);\n   // level 1 (r0 and nx still pending)\n   wire [2*PW-1:0] u0 = csa3(t0[PW-1:0], t0[2*PW-1:PW], t1[PW-1:0]);\n   wire [2*PW-1:0] u1 = csa3(t1[2*PW-1:PW], t2[PW-1:0], t2[2*PW-1:PW]);\n   // level 2\n   wire [2*PW-1:0] v0 = csa3(u0[PW-1:0], u0[2*PW-1:PW], u1[PW-1:0]);\n   wire [2*PW-1:0] v1 = csa3(u1[2*PW-1:PW], r0, nx);\n   // level 3\n   wire [2*PW-1:0] f0 = csa3(v0[PW-1:0], v0[2*PW-1:PW], v1[PW-1:0]);\n   // level 4\n   wire [2*PW-1:0] f1 = csa3(f0[PW-1:0], f0[2*PW-1:PW], v1[2*PW-1:PW]);\n\n   wire [PW-1:0] ps = f1[PW-1:0];\n   wire [PW-1:0] pc = f1[2*PW-1:PW];\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n\t w          <= {DW{1'b0}};\n\t a_east     <= {DW{1'b0}};\n\t psum_south <= {(2*PW){1'b0}};\n      end\n      else begin\n\t if (ld)\n\t   w <= w_north;\n\t if (en) begin\n\t    a_east     <= a_west;\n\t    psum_south <= {pc, ps};\n\t end\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter PW = 19)\n   (\n    input\t\t clk,\n    input\t\t rst,\n    input\t\t ld,\n    input\t\t en,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t w_top,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t a_left,\n    output [N*2*PW-1:0]\t c_bot\n    );\n\n   wire [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [2*PW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar\t   i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n\t assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n\t assign psum_v[0][j] = {(2*PW){1'b0}};\n\t assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n\t assign c_bot[j*2*PW +: 2*PW] = psum_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n\t for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n\t    tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .PW(PW)) u_pe\n\t      (.clk        (clk),\n\t       .rst        (rst),\n\t       .ld         (ld),\n\t       .en         (en),\n\t       .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n\t       .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n\t       .psum_north (psum_v[i][j]),\n\t       .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n\t       .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n\t       .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j]));\n\t end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32,\n    parameter PW = 19)\n   (\n    input\t\t clk,\n    input\t\t rst,\n    input\t\t w_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t w_data,\n    input\t\t a_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t a_data,\n    output\t\t c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\t c_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire\t      ld = w_valid;\n   wire\t      en = ~w_valid;\n\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar\t   i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n\t wire [DW-1:0] din =\n\t\t\ta_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n\t if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n\t    assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n\t end\n\t else begin : g_skewn\n\t    reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n\t    integer k;\n\t    always @(posedge clk) begin\n\t       if (rst) begin\n\t\t  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)\n\t\t    sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n\t       end\n\t       else if (en) begin\n\t\t  sr[0] <= din;\n\t\t  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n\t\t    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n\t       end\n\t    end\n\t    assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n\t end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   wire [N*2*PW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .PW(PW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n\t localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n\t wire [2*PW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*2*PW +: 2*PW];\n\t wire [PW-1:0]   bin = cin[PW-1:0] + cin[2*PW-1:PW];\n\t if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n\t    assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t\t    {{(ACCW-PW){bin[PW-1]}}, bin};\n\t end\n\t else begin : g_deskewn\n\t    reg [PW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n\t    integer k;\n\t    always @(posedge clk) begin\n\t       if (rst) begin\n\t\t  for (k = 0; Tree-C with a cross at the final level:\n// f0 = csa3(v0.s, v1.s, v1.c), f1 = csa3(f0.s, f0.c, v0.c).\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter PW = 19)\n   (\n    input\t\t clk,\n    input\t\t rst,\n    input\t\t ld,\n    input\t\t en,\n    input [DW-1:0]\t w_north,\n    input [DW-1:0]\t a_west,\n    input [2*PW-1:0]\t psum_north,\n    output [DW-1:0]\t w_south,\n    output reg [DW-1:0]\t a_east,\n    output reg [2*PW-1:0] psum_south\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   function [2*PW-1:0] csa3;\n      input [PW-1:0] x, y, z;\n      reg [PW-1:0]   sum, cry;\n      begin\n\t sum = x ^ y ^ z;\n\t cry = ((x & y) | (y & z) | (x & z)) << 1;\n\t csa3 = {cry, sum};\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   wire [PW-1:0] as = {{(PW-DW){a_west[DW-1]}}, a_west};\n   wire [PW-1:0] r0 = w[0] ? as              : {PW{1'b0}};\n   wire [PW-1:0] r1 = w[1] ? (as << 7)       : {PW{1'b0}};\n   wire [PW-1:0] nx = ~ex;\n   wire [PW-1:0] one = {{(PW-1){1'b0}}, 1'b1};\n\n   wire [PW-1:0] ns = psum_north[PW-1:0];\n   wire [PW-1:0] nc = psum_north[2*PW-1:PW];\n\n   // level 0\n   wire [2*PW-1:0] t0 = csa3(r0, r1, r2);\n   wire [2*PW-1:0] t1 = csa3(r3, r4, one);\n   wire [2*PW-1:0] t2 = csa3(r5, r6, nx);\n   // level 1\n   wire [2*PW-1:0] u0 = csa3(t0[PW-1:0], t0[2*PW-1:PW], t1[PW-1:0]);\n   wire [2*PW-1:0] u1 = csa3(t1[2*PW-1:PW], t2[PW-1:0], t2[2*PW-1:PW]);\n   // level 2: psum pair split\n   wire [2*PW-1:0] v0 = csa3(u0[PW-1:0], u0[2*PW-1:PW], ns);\n   wire [2*PW-1:0] v1 = csa3(u1[PW-1:0], u1[2*PW-1:PW], nc);\n   // level 3\n   wire [2*PW-1:0] f0 = csa3(v0[PW-1:0], v0[2*PW-1:PW], v1[PW-1:0]);\n   // level 4\n   wire [2*PW-1:0] f1 = csa3(f0[PW-1:0], f0[2*PW-1:PW], v1[2*PW-1:PW]);\n\n   wire [PW-1:0] ps = f1[PW-1:0];\n   wire [PW-1:0] pc = f1[2*PW-1:PW];\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n\t w          <= {DW{1'b0}};\n\t a_east     <= {DW{1'b0}};\n\t psum_south <= {(2*PW){1'b0}};\n      end\n      else begin\n\t if (ld)\n\t   w <= w_north;\n\t if (en) begin\n\t    a_east     <= a_west;\n\t    psum_south <= {pc, ps};\n\t end\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter PW = 19)\n   (\n    input\t\t clk,\n    input\t\t rst,\n    input\t\t ld,\n    input\t\t en,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t w_top,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t a_left,\n    output [N*2*PW-1:0]\t c_bot\n    );\n\n   wire [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [2*PW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar\t   i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n\t assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n\t assign psum_v[0][j] = {(2*PW){1'b0}};\n\t assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n\t assign c_bot[j*2*PW +: 2*PW] = psum_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n\t for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n\t    tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .PW(PW)) u_pe\n\t      (.clk        (clk),\n\t       .rst        (rst),\n\t       .ld         (ld),\n\t       .en         (en),\n\t       .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n\t       .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n\t       .psum_north (psum_v[i][j]),\n\t       .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n\t       .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n\t       .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j]));\n\t end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32,\n    parameter PW = 19)\n   (\n    input\t\t clk,\n    input\t\t rst,\n    input\t\t w_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t w_data,\n    input\t\t a_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t a_data,\n    output\t\t c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\t c_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire\t      ld = w_valid;\n   wire\t      en = ~w_valid;\n\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar\t   i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n\t wire [DW-1:0] din =\n\t\t\ta_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n\t if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n\t    assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n\t end\n\t else begin : g_skewn\n\t    reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n\t    integer k;\n\t    always @(posedge clk) begin\n\t       if (rst) begin\n\t\t  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)\n\t\t    sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n\t       end\n\t       else if (en) begin\n\t\t  sr[0] <= din;\n\t\t  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n\t\t    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n\t       end\n\t    end\n\t    assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n\t end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   wire [N*2*PW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .PW(PW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n\t assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n\t assign psum_v[0][j] = {(2*PW){1'b0}};\n\t assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n\t assign c_bot[j*2*PW +: 2*PW] = psum_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n\t for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n\t    tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .PW(PW)) u_pe\n\t      (.clk        (clk),\n\t       .rst        (rst),\n\t       .ld         (ld),\n\t       .en         (en),\n\t       .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n\t       .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n\t       .psum_north (psum_v[i][j]),\n\t       .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n\t       .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n\t       .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j]));\n\t end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32,\n    parameter PW = 19)\n   (\n    input\t\t clk,\n    input\t\t rst,\n    input\t\t w_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t w_data,\n    input\t\t a_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t a_data,\n    output\t\t c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\t c_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire\t      ld = w_valid;\n   wire\t      en = ~w_valid;\n\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar\t   i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n\t wire [DW-1:0] din =\n\t\t\ta_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n\t if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n\t    assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n\t end\n\t else begin : g_skewn\n\t    reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n\t    integer k;\n\t    always @(posedge clk) begin\n\t       if (en) begin\n\t\t  sr[0] <= din;\n\t\t  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n\t\t    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n\t       end\n\t    end\n\t    assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n\t end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   wire [N*2*PW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .PW(PW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n\t localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n\t wire [2*PW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*2*PW +: 2*PW];\n\t wire [PW-1:0]   bin = cin[PW-1:0] + cin[2*PW-1:PW];\n\t if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n\t    assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t\t    {{(ACCW-PW){bin[PW-1]}}, bin};\n\t end\n\t else begin : g_deskewn\n\t    reg [PW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n\t    integer k;\n\t    always @(posedge clk) begin\n\t       if (en) begin\n\t\t  dr[0] <= bin;\n\t\t  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n\t\t    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n\t       end\n\t    end\n\t    assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t\t    {{(ACCW-PW){dr[D-1][PW-1]}}, dr[D-1]};\n\t end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n\tvpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n\tvpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design22.sv')

Final verification complete — **design19.sv is the winner, reproducible at ADP 2,367,703** with 0/6782 testbench errors.

## Summary

**Best design: `design19.sv`** — ADP **2,367,703** (area 4793.6, delay 493.9 ps), a **49% ADP reduction** vs the starting point (4,624,409 / area 4604 / delay 1004 ps), verified cycle-accurate against all 6782 golden-trace checks (c_valid every cycle, c_data on every valid cycle — including stalls, zero-injection, a_valid gaps, and mid-drain weight reloads).

### How it got there (each step verified + measured)

1. **19-bit accumulators** (design.sv): every psum in the mesh holds at most 8 products of 8×8-signed operands (fits 19 signed bits), so the 32-bit datapath's upper bits were pure sign — recovered exactly by sign extension. ADP → 3.24M.
2. **Carry-save psums** (design2): each PE stores a redundant {carry,sum} pair, replacing a 19-bit ripple add with one XOR3/MAJ level; the CPA moved to the column outputs. ADP → 3.10M.
3. **Manual compressor tree, no `*`** (design4): the product is built as shifted rows `a·w = Σₖ w[k]·(a≪k) − w[7]·(a≪7)` (with `−ex = ~ex+1` folded in), and the incoming psum pair is ingested directly into one balanced 5-level 3:2 compressor tree — the PE stage contains **zero carry-propagate adders**. ADP → 2.75M.
4. **Reset on the D-path** (design5): async reset removes the reset mux from every FF's data path. ADP → 2.65M.
5. **Aggressive timing mapping** (target_delay ≤ 400): area is FF-dominated but delay kept improving; the ≤400 bin maps to the fastest netlist. ADP → 2.52M.
6. **Structure/reset-style exploration** (designs 6–22): compressor-tree operand grouping swings mapping area/delay by ±5%; the winner is the grouping with the constant row paired with the two low rows `(r0, r1, +1)`, the negated bit-7 row `(r5, r6, ~ex)` last, and the psum pair split across the two level-2 compressors, with the valid pipeline as the only reset register — **ADP 2.368M** (design19).

The systolic mesh itself (input skew, per-column output de-skew, ld/en stall semantics, 15-cycle valid pipeline) is preserved exactly, which is what makes the mid-stream weight-reload mixing behavior match the golden design for free.

**Journey from baseline 4,624,409 → 2,367,703 (−49%):**

| Step | Innovation | ADP |
|---|---|---|
| baseline | original logikbench RTL (32-bit systolic mesh) | 4.62M |
| 1 | 19-bit accumulators (8 products of 8×8 signed always fit; sign-extend to 32-bit output) | 3.24M |
| 2 | carry-save {sum,carry} psums — CPA out of the PE clock path | 3.10M |
| 3 | manual partial-product compressor tree, no `*`, psum pair folded into the same tree — **zero CPAs in the array** | 2.75M |
| 4 | reset style: async reset removes the D-path mux (later: none on datapath) | 2.65M |
| 5 | aggressive timing mapping (target ≤ 400 ps plateau) | 2.52M |
| 6 | compressor-tree operand-grouping search (16 variants measured) | **2.368M** |

The winning PE: 7 positive shifted rows + `~ex` (bit-7 negation via two's complement, constant `+1` folded into the `(r0,r1,+1)` compressor), psum pair split across the two level-2 compressors; 5 XOR3/MAJ3 levels total — the proven minimum for 11 operands. The systolic mesh (skew, de-skew, stall semantics, 15-cycle valid pipeline, mid-drain weight-reload mixing) is untouched, which is exactly what preserves bit- and cycle-accuracy.

# Design Session Summary — `tpu` weight-stationary MXU tile

**Final: `design19.sv` — ADP 2.368e6 (area 4793.6, delay 493.9 ps), 0/6782 errors**, vs baseline 4.624e6 (**−49%**).

## 1. Approaches tried / what worked

Kept the original systolic mesh architecture throughout (skew/de-skew/stall semantics are hard to replicate exactly with mid-drain weight-reload mixing), and optimized the PE datapath + mapping:

- **Narrow accumulators** (`design.sv`): every psum holds ≤ 8 signed products → 19 bits suffice; 32-bit output recovered by sign extension. → 3.24M
- **Carry-save psums** (`design2.sv`): PE stores redundant {carry,sum}; per-stage CPA replaced by one XOR3/MAJ level. → 3.10M
- **Manual compressor tree, no `*`** (`design4.sv`): product built from shifted rows `a·w = Σₖ w[k]·(a≪k) − w[7]·(a≪7)` (`−ex = ~ex+1`), psum pair fed directly into the same balanced 5-level 3:2 tree → **zero CPAs anywhere in the array**. → 2.75M
- **Reset/mapping/grouping search** (designs 5–22, ~16 variants): reset style + tree operand grouping change abc's mapping by ±5%. Winner: `design19.sv` = const row paired with low rows `(r0,r1,+1)`, negated row `(r5,r6,~ex)` last, psum pair split across the two level-2 compressors, datapath FFs without reset (vpipe keeps reset) — **2.368M**

## 2. Highest-impact optimizations

1. **Manual CSA tree replacing `$signed*` + adder** (~−15% ADP each): removed both the multiplier's internal CPA and the accumulator adder from the clock-critical path (delay 820→581 ps). The 32→19-bit narrowing and carry-save were the other big wins.
2. **target_delay ≤ 400**: the achieved-delay × area product is best in the most aggressive mapping bin (500/570 bins were worse); area barely moves, delay drops ~30 ps.
3. **Grouping/reset lottery** (~−2% more): cheap to test, surprisingly large mapping variance (±5%).

## 3. What didn't work (regressions)

- Explicit unsigned-mul + sign correction (`design3`, 3.43M) — Yosys's native `$signed` mul is more compact.
- Psum-at-level-0/level-1 groupings, L1/L3 cross-pairings, Booth radix-4 (recode + popcount depth eats the tree-level savings) — all 2.5–2.7M.
- Removing datapath resets sometimes *increased* area (mapping noise); async-vs-sync-vs-none ordering changed with tree shape — no universal winner, only per-combination luck.
- Per-row accumulator narrowing: mathematically valid but blocked — widening a *redundant* pair mod 2ᵂ isn't possible without a CPA.
- target_delay 570/800: delay rose far more than area fell.

## 4. Lessons

- **Structure you hand to the mapper matters as much as the math**: identical-function CSA chains (11 variants) spanned 2.37–2.67M. Budget lottery tickets after finding the best *architecture*.
- The metric rewards pushing timing far past feasibility when area is FF-dominated (area is nearly constant across target bins).
- Verify bounds rigorously before narrowing (19 bits, not 18: all-`0x80` tiles hit exactly 2¹⁷).
- Next time: build the CSA tree + narrow widths + manual rows from the start (skip the `$signed*` detour), then spend the budget on grouping/reset exploration with `target_delay=300`.
